+}
+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+fn snapshot_value(bank: &Bank, identity: &Result<Identity, String>) -> Answer {
+    match identity {
+        Ok(identity) => match bank
+            .lock()
+            .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner())
+            .get(&identity.key)
+            .cloned()
+        {
+            Some(plan) => Answer::Document(plan.document.clone()),
+            None => Answer::Text(format!("no plan published for {}", identity.name)),
+        },
+        Err(why) => Answer::Refused(why.clone()),
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+fn publish_snapshot(publisher: &panel_control::windows_services::PlanPublisher, answer: Answer) {
+    let result = match answer {
+        Answer::Document(document) => publisher.publish_document(document),
+        Answer::Text(text) => publisher.publish_text(text),
+        Answer::Refused(message) => publisher.publish_failed(message),
+        _ => return,
+    };
+    if let Err(problem) = result {
+        eprintln!("Windows plan snapshot was not updated: {problem}");
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+fn publish_matching_snapshots(bank: &Bank, snapshots: &SnapshotReaders, key: &Key) {
+    let matches = snapshots
+        .lock()
+        .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner())
+        .values()
+        .filter(|(identity, _)| identity.as_ref().is_ok_and(|identity| &identity.key == key))
+        .cloned()
+        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
+    for (identity, publisher) in matches {
+        publish_snapshot(&publisher, snapshot_value(bank, &identity));
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+fn publish_all_snapshots(bank: &Bank, snapshots: &SnapshotReaders) {
+    let all = snapshots
+        .lock()
+        .unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner())
+        .values()
+        .cloned()
+        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
+    for (identity, publisher) in all {
+        publish_snapshot(&publisher, snapshot_value(bank, &identity));
     }
 }

+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+#[test]
+fn windows_control_plan_snapshots_follow_commit_retire_and_revoke_off_frame() {
+    let mut store = Store::at(None, false, eframe::egui::Context::default());
+    let identity = identity();
+    let (first_reader, first_publisher) = panel_control::windows_services::PlanSnapshot::new();
+    let (second_reader, second_publisher) = panel_control::windows_services::PlanSnapshot::new();
+    store.install_snapshot(11, Ok(identity.clone()), first_publisher.clone());
+    store.install_snapshot(12, Ok(identity.clone()), second_publisher.clone());
+    for reader in [&first_reader, &second_reader] {
+        assert!(matches!(
+            reader.read(),
+            Ok(panel_control::Reply::Text(text)) if text == "no plan published for Sample"
+        ));
+    }
+
+    let document = r#"{"title":"Live","milestones":[]}"#;
+    let ctx = eframe::egui::Context::default();
+    let (answer, received) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
+    store.request(Ok(identity.clone()), set(document), answer, &ctx, None);
+    assert!(matches!(received.recv().unwrap(), Answer::Text(_)));
+    for reader in [&first_reader, &second_reader] {
+        assert_eq!(
+            reader.read().unwrap(),
+            panel_control::Reply::Document(document.to_owned())
+        );
+    }
+
+    let (answer, received) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
+    store.request(Ok(identity), What::PlanRetire, answer, &ctx, None);
+    assert!(matches!(received.recv().unwrap(), Answer::Text(_)));
+    for reader in [&first_reader, &second_reader] {
+        assert!(matches!(
+            reader.read(),
+            Ok(panel_control::Reply::Text(text)) if text == "no plan published for Sample"
+        ));
+    }
+
+    first_publisher.revoke();
+    store.retain_snapshots(&[12]);
+    assert_eq!(store.snapshots.lock().unwrap().len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(first_reader.read().unwrap_err(), "this pane is gone");
+}
+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+#[test]
+fn windows_control_plan_snapshot_registration_cannot_miss_a_concurrent_commit() {
+    let store = Arc::new(Store::default());
+    let identity = identity();
+    let (reader, publisher) = panel_control::windows_services::PlanSnapshot::new();
+    let barrier = Arc::new(std::sync::Barrier::new(2));
+    let registering = {
+        let store = Arc::clone(&store);
+        let identity = identity.clone();
+        let publisher = publisher.clone();
+        let barrier = Arc::clone(&barrier);
+        std::thread::spawn(move || {
+            barrier.wait();
+            store.install_snapshot(21, Ok(identity), publisher);
+        })
+    };
+    let committing = {
+        let bank = store.bank.clone();
+        let snapshots = store.snapshots.clone();
+        let identity = identity.clone();
+        let barrier = Arc::clone(&barrier);
+        std::thread::spawn(move || {
+            barrier.wait();
+            let mut disk = disk::Disk::new(None, false);
+            apply(
+                &bank,
+                &mut disk,
+                &identity,
+                set(r#"{"title":"Raced","milestones":[]}"#),
+            );
+            publish_matching_snapshots(&bank, &snapshots, &identity.key);
+        })
+    };
+    registering.join().unwrap();
+    committing.join().unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(
+        reader.read().unwrap(),
+        panel_control::Reply::Document(r#"{"title":"Raced","milestones":[]}"#.to_owned())
+    );
+}

+pub(crate) fn control_hwnd(ctx: &egui::Context) -> Option<isize> {
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    {
+        ctx.data(|data| data.get_temp(egui::Id::new("ivy-panel-control-hwnd")))
+    }
+    #[cfg(not(windows))]
+    {
+        let _ = ctx;
+        None
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
+mod windows_control_tests {
+    use super::*;
+    use std::ptr::{null, null_mut};
+
+    #[test]
+    fn windows_control_native_wake_posts_without_waiting_for_dispatch() {
+        use windows_sys::Win32::{
+            System::LibraryLoader::GetModuleHandleW,
+            UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
+                CreateWindowExW, DefWindowProcW, DestroyWindow, MSG, PM_REMOVE, PeekMessageW,
+                RegisterClassW, UnregisterClassW, WM_PAINT, WNDCLASSW,
+            },
+        };
+
+        let class_name: Vec<u16> = format!("IvyControlWake{}", std::process::id())
+            .encode_utf16()
+            .chain(std::iter::once(0))
+            .collect();
+        let instance = unsafe { GetModuleHandleW(null()) };
+        let class = WNDCLASSW {
+            lpfnWndProc: Some(DefWindowProcW),
+            hInstance: instance,
+            lpszClassName: class_name.as_ptr(),
+            ..Default::default()
+        };
+        assert_ne!(
+            unsafe { RegisterClassW(&class) },
+            0,
+            "register native test window"
+        );
+        let hwnd = unsafe {
+            CreateWindowExW(
+                0,
+                class_name.as_ptr(),
+                class_name.as_ptr(),
+                0,
+                0,
+                0,
+                0,
+                0,
+                (-3isize) as _,
+                null_mut(),
+                instance,
+                null(),
+            )
+        };
+        assert!(!hwnd.is_null(), "create native message window");
+        let wake = panel_control::WindowsWake::from_hwnd(hwnd as isize).unwrap();
+        wake.post().expect("post native wake without dispatch");
+        let mut message = MSG::default();
+        assert_ne!(
+            unsafe { PeekMessageW(&mut message, hwnd, WM_PAINT, WM_PAINT, PM_REMOVE) },
+            0,
+            "the posted WM_PAINT is queued while the window thread has not dispatched it"
+        );
+        wake.revoke();
+        assert!(wake.post().is_err(), "revoked HWND rejects later posts");
+        unsafe {
+            DestroyWindow(hwnd);
+            UnregisterClassW(class_name.as_ptr(), instance);
+        }
+    }
+}

+
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    pub(crate) fn install_windows_document_publisher(
+        &mut self,
+        publisher: panel_control::windows_services::DocumentPublisher,
+    ) {
+        self.windows_document = Some(publisher);
+        self.publish_windows_document(self.last_good.as_deref());
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    fn publish_windows_document(&self, panel: Option<&Panel>) {
+        let Some(publisher) = &self.windows_document else {
+            return;
+        };
+        let result = match panel {
+            Some(panel) => match panel_contract::serialize(panel) {
+                Ok(document) => publisher.publish(document),
+                Err(problem) => publisher.publish_failed(problem.to_string()),
+            },
+            None => publisher.clear(),
+        };
+        if let Err(problem) = result {
+            eprintln!("Windows panel snapshot was not updated: {problem}");
+        }
+    }

+#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
+mod windows_control_tests {
+    use super::*;
+
+    #[test]
+    fn windows_control_document_snapshot_tracks_commit_clear_and_failed_serialize() {
+        let (reader, publisher) = panel_control::windows_services::DocumentSnapshot::new();
+        let mut state = PanelState::default();
+        state.install_windows_document_publisher(publisher);
+        let panel = panel_contract::parse_document(r#"{"lines":["committed"]}"#).unwrap();
+        let canonical = panel_contract::serialize(&panel).unwrap();
+        state.commit(panel);
+        assert_eq!(reader.read().unwrap(), canonical);
+
+        clear(&mut state);
+        assert_eq!(
+            reader.read().unwrap_err(),
+            NOTHING_PUBLISHED,
+            "retirement clears the snapshot instead of leaving a prior commit"
+        );
+
+        let mut too_large = panel_contract::parse_document(r#"{"lines":["valid"]}"#).unwrap();
+        too_large.phase = Some("x".repeat(panel_contract::MAX_BYTES as usize));
+        assert!(panel_contract::serialize(&too_large).is_err());
+        state.commit(too_large);
+        assert!(
+            reader.read().unwrap_err() != NOTHING_PUBLISHED,
+            "an unrepresentable commit reports its serialization failure, not a stale document"
+        );
+    }
 }

+/// The server process witness is a separate Windows launch variable, not part of the stable
+/// two-entry panel identity contract. Sending `None` explicitly clears an inherited parent value.
+#[cfg(windows)]
+pub(crate) fn server_pid_env(
+    panel: Option<&PanelDir>,
+) -> (&'static str, Option<std::ffi::OsString>) {
+    (
+        "PANEL_SERVER_PID",
+        panel.map(|_| std::process::id().to_string().into()),
+    )
+}
+
+#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
+mod windows_control_tests {
+    use super::*;
+
+    #[test]
+    fn windows_control_server_pid_is_separate_and_cleared_without_a_panel() {
+        let dir = PanelDir::create().expect("private panel directory");
+        let (name, pid) = server_pid_env(Some(&dir));
+        let expected = std::process::id().to_string();
+        assert_eq!(name, "PANEL_SERVER_PID");
+        assert_eq!(
+            pid.as_deref().and_then(std::ffi::OsStr::to_str),
+            Some(expected.as_str())
+        );
+        let (name, pid) = server_pid_env(None);
+        assert_eq!(name, "PANEL_SERVER_PID");
+        assert!(
+            pid.is_none(),
+            "a no-panel launch clears an inherited server PID"
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            identity_of(None).map(|(name, _)| name),
+            panel_contract::ENV_NAMES
+        );
+    }
+}
+

diff --git a/crates/panel-contract/src/windows_directory.rs b/crates/panel-contract/src/windows_directory.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b86052d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/panel-contract/src/windows_directory.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,445 @@
+//! Windows creation and validation for private pane directories.
+//!
+//! The ACL is applied in `CreateDirectoryW`, so no permissive directory is visible between
+//! creation and tightening. Inherited paths are opened without following reparse points and their
+//! owner, protected DACL, type, and exact allow entries are checked through that handle.
+
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::ffi::c_void;
+use std::io;
+use std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::ptr::{null, null_mut};
+
+use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::Security::Authorization::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::Security::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::Storage::FileSystem::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::System::Threading::*;
+
+fn error() -> io::Error {
+    io::Error::last_os_error()
+}
+fn wide(value: &OsStr) -> Vec<u16> {
+    value.encode_wide().chain(Some(0)).collect()
+}
+
+struct OwnedHandle(HANDLE);
+impl Drop for OwnedHandle {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        unsafe {
+            CloseHandle(self.0);
+        }
+    }
+}
+struct OwnedDescriptor(PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR);
+impl Drop for OwnedDescriptor {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        unsafe {
+            LocalFree(self.0);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+fn token_user() -> io::Result<(OwnedHandle, Vec<usize>)> {
+    let mut token = null_mut();
+    if unsafe { OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_QUERY, &mut token) } == 0 {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    let token = OwnedHandle(token);
+    let mut needed = 0;
+    unsafe {
+        GetTokenInformation(token.0, TokenUser, null_mut(), 0, &mut needed);
+    }
+    let mut bytes = vec![0usize; (needed as usize).div_ceil(std::mem::size_of::<usize>())];
+    if unsafe {
+        GetTokenInformation(
+            token.0,
+            TokenUser,
+            bytes.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
+            needed,
+            &mut needed,
+        )
+    } == 0
+    {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    Ok((token, bytes))
+}
+
+fn descriptor(children: bool) -> io::Result<OwnedDescriptor> {
+    let (_token, user) = token_user()?;
+    let token_user = unsafe { &*(user.as_ptr().cast::<TOKEN_USER>()) };
+    let mut sid = null_mut();
+    if unsafe { ConvertSidToStringSidW(token_user.User.Sid, &mut sid) } == 0 {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    let mut length = 0;
+    unsafe {
+        while *sid.add(length) != 0 {
+            length += 1;
+        }
+    }
+    let user_sid = String::from_utf16_lossy(unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(sid, length) });
+    unsafe {
+        LocalFree(sid.cast());
+    }
+    let inherit = if children { "OICI" } else { "" };
+    let system_ace = if user_sid == "S-1-5-18" {
+        String::new()
+    } else {
+        format!("(A;{inherit};FA;;;SY)")
+    };
+    let sddl: Vec<u16> = format!("O:{user_sid}D:P(A;{inherit};FA;;;{user_sid}){system_ace}")
+        .encode_utf16()
+        .chain(Some(0))
+        .collect();
+    let mut output = null_mut();
+    let mut size = 0;
+    if unsafe {
+        ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW(
+            sddl.as_ptr(),
+            SDDL_REVISION_1,
+            &mut output,
+            &mut size,
+        )
+    } == 0
+    {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    Ok(OwnedDescriptor(output))
+}
+
+pub fn create(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+    let security = descriptor(true)?;
+    let attributes = SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES {
+        nLength: std::mem::size_of::<SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES>() as u32,
+        lpSecurityDescriptor: security.0,
+        bInheritHandle: 0,
+    };
+    let path = wide(path.as_os_str());
+    if unsafe { CreateDirectoryW(path.as_ptr(), &attributes) } == 0 {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+pub fn validate(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+    let path_wide = no_follow_path(path.as_os_str());
+    let handle = unsafe {
+        CreateFileW(
+            path_wide.as_ptr(),
+            READ_CONTROL | FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES,
+            FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE | FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
+            null(),
+            OPEN_EXISTING,
+            FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS | FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
+            null_mut(),
+        )
+    };
+    if handle == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    let handle = OwnedHandle(handle);
+    let mut info = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed::<BY_HANDLE_FILE_INFORMATION>() };
+    if unsafe { GetFileInformationByHandle(handle.0, &mut info) } == 0 {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    if info.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY == 0
+        || info.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT != 0
+    {
+        return Err(io::Error::new(
+            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+            format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+        ));
+    }
+    let (token, token_bytes) = token_user()?;
+    let user = unsafe { &*(token_bytes.as_ptr().cast::<TOKEN_USER>()) };
+    let mut owner = null_mut();
+    let mut dacl = null_mut();
+    let mut descriptor = null_mut();
+    let result = unsafe {
+        GetSecurityInfo(
+            handle.0,
+            SE_FILE_OBJECT,
+            OWNER_SECURITY_INFORMATION | DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION,
+            &mut owner,
+            null_mut(),
+            &mut dacl,
+            null_mut(),
+            &mut descriptor,
+        )
+    };
+    if result != ERROR_SUCCESS {
+        return Err(io::Error::from_raw_os_error(result as i32));
+    }
+    let descriptor = OwnedDescriptor(descriptor);
+    if owner.is_null() || dacl.is_null() || unsafe { EqualSid(owner, user.User.Sid) } == 0 {
+        return Err(io::Error::new(
+            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+            format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+        ));
+    }
+    let mut control = 0;
+    let mut revision = 0;
+    if unsafe { GetSecurityDescriptorControl(descriptor.0, &mut control, &mut revision) } == 0
+        || control & SE_DACL_PROTECTED == 0
+    {
+        return Err(io::Error::new(
+            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+            format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+        ));
+    }
+    let mut acl_info = ACL_SIZE_INFORMATION {
+        AceCount: 0,
+        AclBytesInUse: 0,
+        AclBytesFree: 0,
+    };
+    if unsafe {
+        GetAclInformation(
+            dacl,
+            (&mut acl_info as *mut ACL_SIZE_INFORMATION).cast(),
+            std::mem::size_of::<ACL_SIZE_INFORMATION>() as u32,
+            AclSizeInformation,
+        )
+    } == 0
+    {
+        return Err(error());
+    }
+    #[repr(align(4))]
+    struct AlignedSid([u8; 12]);
+    let mut system_sid = AlignedSid([1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 5, 18, 0, 0, 0]);
+    let system_sid_ptr = system_sid.0.as_mut_ptr().cast::<c_void>();
+    // S-1-5-18 is the fixed LocalSystem SID representation.
+    let system_is_user = unsafe { EqualSid(user.User.Sid, system_sid_ptr) } != 0;
+    if acl_info.AceCount != if system_is_user { 1 } else { 2 } {
+        return Err(io::Error::new(
+            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+            format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+        ));
+    }
+    let expected_flags = (OBJECT_INHERIT_ACE | CONTAINER_INHERIT_ACE) as u8;
+    let mut user_found = false;
+    let mut system_found = false;
+    for index in 0..acl_info.AceCount {
+        let mut ace = null_mut();
+        if unsafe { GetAce(dacl, index, &mut ace) } == 0 {
+            return Err(error());
+        }
+        let header = unsafe { &*(ace.cast::<ACE_HEADER>()) };
+        if header.AceType != 0 || header.AceFlags != expected_flags {
+            return Err(io::Error::new(
+                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+                format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+            ));
+        }
+        let allow = unsafe { &*(ace.cast::<ACCESS_ALLOWED_ACE>()) };
+        if allow.Mask != FILE_ALL_ACCESS {
+            return Err(io::Error::new(
+                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+                format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+            ));
+        }
+        let sid_ptr = (&allow.SidStart as *const u32).cast_mut().cast::<c_void>();
+        if unsafe { EqualSid(sid_ptr, user.User.Sid) } != 0 {
+            user_found = true;
+        } else if unsafe { EqualSid(sid_ptr, system_sid_ptr) } != 0 {
+            system_found = true;
+        } else {
+            return Err(io::Error::new(
+                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+                format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+    let _ = token;
+    if !user_found || (!system_is_user && !system_found) {
+        return Err(io::Error::new(
+            io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+            format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", path.display()),
+        ));
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}
+
+fn no_follow_path(path: &OsStr) -> Vec<u16> {
+    let mut units: Vec<u16> = path.encode_wide().collect();
+    loop {
+        let len = units.len();
+        if len > 3 && (units[len - 1] == b'\\' as u16 || units[len - 1] == b'/' as u16) {
+            units.pop();
+        } else if len > 3
+            && units[len - 1] == b'.' as u16
+            && (units[len - 2] == b'\\' as u16 || units[len - 2] == b'/' as u16)
+        {
+            units.truncate(len - 2);
+        } else {
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    units.push(0);
+    units
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    use super::*;
+
+    #[test]
+    fn fresh_panel_directory_has_the_required_owner_and_protected_acl() {
+        let panel = crate::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        validate(panel.path()).unwrap();
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn inherited_file_and_missing_path_are_rejected() {
+        let panel = crate::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let file = panel.path().join("ordinary-file");
+        std::fs::write(&file, "x").unwrap();
+        assert!(validate(&file).is_err());
+        assert!(validate(&panel.path().join("missing")).is_err());
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn inherited_reparse_point_is_rejected_even_with_terminal_dot_or_separator() {
+        let panel = crate::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let target = panel.path().with_extension("target");
+        std::fs::create_dir(&target).unwrap();
+        let alias = panel.path().with_extension("alias");
+        let junction = format!("mklink /J \"{}\" \"{}\"", alias.display(), target.display());
+        let status = std::process::Command::new("cmd.exe")
+            .args(["/d", "/c", &junction])
+            .status()
+            .unwrap();
+        assert!(
+            status.success(),
+            "could not create junction fixture: {junction}"
+        );
+        for suffix in ["", "\\", "\\.", "\\.\\.\\"] {
+            let candidate = std::path::PathBuf::from(format!("{}{suffix}", alias.display()));
+            assert!(
+                validate(&candidate).is_err(),
+                "accepted reparse path {}",
+                candidate.display()
+            );
+        }
+        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir(&alias);
+        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir(&target);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn null_and_broad_dacls_are_rejected() {
+        let panel = crate::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        set_test_dacl(panel.path(), None, true);
+        assert!(validate(panel.path()).is_err(), "accepted a null DACL");
+        let panel = crate::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        set_test_dacl(panel.path(), Some("D:(A;;FA;;;WD)"), true);
+        assert!(validate(panel.path()).is_err(), "accepted a broad DACL");
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn otherwise_exact_but_unprotected_directory_dacl_is_rejected() {
+        let panel = crate::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let (_token, user) = token_user().unwrap();
+        let user = unsafe { &*(user.as_ptr().cast::<TOKEN_USER>()) };
+        let mut sid = null_mut();
+        assert_ne!(
+            unsafe { ConvertSidToStringSidW(user.User.Sid, &mut sid) },
+            0
+        );
+        let mut length = 0;
+        unsafe {
+            while *sid.add(length) != 0 {
+                length += 1;
+            }
+        }
+        let user_sid = String::from_utf16_lossy(unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(sid, length) });
+        unsafe {
+            LocalFree(sid.cast());
+        }
+        let system_ace = if user_sid == "S-1-5-18" {
+            String::new()
+        } else {
+            "(A;OICI;FA;;;SY)".to_owned()
+        };
+        let sddl = format!("D:(A;OICI;FA;;;{user_sid}){system_ace}");
+        set_test_dacl(panel.path(), Some(&sddl), false);
+        assert!(
+            validate(panel.path()).is_err(),
+            "accepted the right allow ACEs on an unprotected DACL"
+        );
+    }
+
+    fn set_test_dacl(path: &Path, sddl: Option<&str>, protected: bool) {
+        let path_wide = wide(path.as_os_str());
+        let handle = unsafe {
+            CreateFileW(
+                path_wide.as_ptr(),
+                READ_CONTROL | WRITE_DAC,
+                FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE | FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
+                null(),
+                OPEN_EXISTING,
+                FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS | FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT,
+                null_mut(),
+            )
+        };
+        assert_ne!(handle, INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE);
+        let handle = OwnedHandle(handle);
+        let descriptor = if let Some(sddl) = sddl {
+            let descriptor_sddl: Vec<u16> = sddl.encode_utf16().chain(Some(0)).collect();
+            let mut descriptor = null_mut();
+            let mut size = 0;
+            assert_ne!(
+                unsafe {
+                    ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW(
+                        descriptor_sddl.as_ptr(),
+                        SDDL_REVISION_1,
+                        &mut descriptor,
+                        &mut size,
+                    )
+                },
+                0
+            );
+            Some(OwnedDescriptor(descriptor))
+        } else {
+            None
+        };
+        let acl = if let Some(descriptor) = &descriptor {
+            let mut present = 0;
+            let mut dacl_defaulted = 0;
+            let mut dacl = null_mut();
+            assert_ne!(
+                unsafe {
+                    GetSecurityDescriptorDacl(
+                        descriptor.0,
+                        &mut present,
+                        &mut dacl,
+                        &mut dacl_defaulted,
+                    )
+                },
+                0
+            );
+            assert_ne!(present, 0);
+            dacl
+        } else {
+            null_mut()
+        };
+        let protection = if protected {
+            PROTECTED_DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION
+        } else {
+            UNPROTECTED_DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION
+        };
+        let result = unsafe {
+            SetSecurityInfo(
+                handle.0,
+                SE_FILE_OBJECT,
+                DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION | protection,
+                null_mut(),
+                null_mut(),
+                acl,
+                null_mut(),
+            )
+        };
+        assert_eq!(result, ERROR_SUCCESS);
+    }
+}

+/// Parses the app identity separately from the stable two-variable pane address.
+pub fn server_pid(value: Option<&std::ffi::OsStr>) -> io::Result<u32> {
+    value
+        .and_then(std::ffi::OsStr::to_str)
+        .filter(|text| !text.is_empty() && text.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit()))
+        .and_then(|text| text.parse::<u32>().ok())
+        .filter(|pid| *pid != 0)
+        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
+            "PANEL_SERVER_PID must be the nonzero decimal PID inherited from Ivy; check how your client forwards it"))
+}
+
+#[cfg(windows)]
+pub(crate) fn inherited_server_pid() -> io::Result<u32> {
+    server_pid(std::env::var_os("PANEL_SERVER_PID").as_deref())
+}

+/// Connects only after the pipe server process matches the inherited app PID.

+    stream.verify_server_pid(expected_pid)?;
+    Ok(stream)

diff --git a/crates/panel-control/src/endpoint/windows.rs b/crates/panel-control/src/endpoint/windows.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1abf07a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/panel-control/src/endpoint/windows.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,876 @@
+//! Native, overlapped named-pipe transport.
+//!
+//! Every operation keeps its `OVERLAPPED` and event alive through cancellation completion. The
+//! only waits here are kernel waits; the caller's receive/send deadlines bound them.
+
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::io::{self, Read, Write};
+use std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt;
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::ptr::{null, null_mut};
+use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicUsize, Ordering};
+use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
+use std::time::Duration;
+
+use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::Security::Authorization::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::Security::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::Storage::FileSystem::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::System::IO::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::System::Pipes::*;
+use windows_sys::Win32::System::Threading::*;
+
+const PIPE_PATH_PREFIX: &str = r"\\.\pipe\";
+const PIPE_MODE: NAMED_PIPE_MODE =
+    PIPE_TYPE_BYTE | PIPE_READMODE_BYTE | PIPE_WAIT | PIPE_REJECT_REMOTE_CLIENTS;
+const PIPE_OPEN: FILE_FLAGS_AND_ATTRIBUTES = FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED | FILE_FLAG_FIRST_PIPE_INSTANCE;
+const PIPE_OPEN_NEXT: FILE_FLAGS_AND_ATTRIBUTES = FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED;
+
+fn winerr() -> io::Error {
+    io::Error::last_os_error()
+}
+fn wide(s: &OsStr) -> Vec<u16> {
+    s.encode_wide().chain(Some(0)).collect()
+}
+
+struct Handle(HANDLE);
+unsafe impl Send for Handle {}
+unsafe impl Sync for Handle {}
+impl Handle {
+    fn new(handle: HANDLE) -> io::Result<Self> {
+        if handle == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE || handle.is_null() {
+            Err(winerr())
+        } else {
+            Ok(Self(handle))
+        }
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for Handle {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        unsafe {
+            CloseHandle(self.0);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct Security(*mut std::ffi::c_void);
+impl Security {
+    fn pipe() -> io::Result<Self> {
+        descriptor(false)
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for Security {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        unsafe {
+            LocalFree(self.0);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+fn descriptor(children: bool) -> io::Result<Security> {
+    let sid = current_sid_string()?;
+    let inherit = if children { "OICI" } else { "" };
+    let system_ace = if sid == "S-1-5-18" {
+        String::new()
+    } else {
+        format!("(A;{inherit};FA;;;SY)")
+    };
+    let sddl: Vec<u16> = format!("O:{sid}D:P(A;{inherit};FA;;;{sid}){system_ace}")
+        .encode_utf16()
+        .chain(Some(0))
+        .collect();
+    let mut raw = null_mut();
+    let mut size = 0;
+    // SAFETY: the SDDL is terminated and output pointers are valid.
+    if unsafe {
+        ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW(
+            sddl.as_ptr(),
+            SDDL_REVISION_1,
+            &mut raw,
+            &mut size,
+        )
+    } == 0
+    {
+        return Err(winerr());
+    }
+    Ok(Security(raw))
+}
+
+fn current_sid_string() -> io::Result<String> {
+    let mut token = null_mut();
+    // SAFETY: process pseudo-handle and output pointer are valid.
+    if unsafe { OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_QUERY, &mut token) } == 0 {
+        return Err(winerr());
+    }
+    let token = Handle(token);
+    let mut needed = 0;
+    // SAFETY: probing the required buffer size is the documented call pattern.
+    unsafe {
+        GetTokenInformation(token.0, TokenUser, null_mut(), 0, &mut needed);
+    }
+    let mut buffer = vec![0usize; (needed as usize).div_ceil(std::mem::size_of::<usize>())];
+    // SAFETY: buffer has the reported size and is suitably aligned for TOKEN_USER.
+    if unsafe {
+        GetTokenInformation(
+            token.0,
+            TokenUser,
+            buffer.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
+            needed,
+            &mut needed,
+        )
+    } == 0
+    {
+        return Err(winerr());
+    }
+    let user = unsafe { &*(buffer.as_ptr().cast::<TOKEN_USER>()) };
+    let mut sid_string = null_mut();
+    // SAFETY: token information owns a valid SID for the duration of this call.
+    if unsafe { ConvertSidToStringSidW(user.User.Sid, &mut sid_string) } == 0 {
+        return Err(winerr());
+    }
+    let mut len = 0;
+    unsafe {
+        while *sid_string.add(len) != 0 {
+            len += 1;
+        }
+    }
+    let result = String::from_utf16_lossy(unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(sid_string, len) });
+    unsafe {
+        LocalFree(sid_string.cast());
+    }
+    Ok(result)
+}
+
+struct ListenerInner {
+    name: Vec<u16>,
+    security: Security,
+    handle: Option<Handle>,
+    first: bool,
+}
+pub struct Listener {
+    inner: Arc<Mutex<ListenerInner>>,
+}
+unsafe impl Send for Listener {}
+unsafe impl Sync for Listener {}
+
+fn new_pipe(name: &[u16], security: &Security, first: bool) -> io::Result<Handle> {
+    let attributes = SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES {
+        nLength: std::mem::size_of::<SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES>() as u32,
+        lpSecurityDescriptor: security.0,
+        bInheritHandle: 0,
+    };
+    // SAFETY: all arguments point to live data, descriptor is explicit, and the resulting handle is overlapped.
+    Handle::new(unsafe {
+        CreateNamedPipeW(
+            name.as_ptr(),
+            PIPE_ACCESS_DUPLEX | if first { PIPE_OPEN } else { PIPE_OPEN_NEXT },
+            PIPE_MODE,
+            PIPE_UNLIMITED_INSTANCES,
+            64 * 1024,
+            64 * 1024,
+            0,
+            &attributes,
+        )
+    })
+}
+
+pub fn bind(_dir: &Path, id: &str) -> io::Result<Listener> {
+    if id.is_empty() || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
+        return Err(io::Error::new(
+            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
+            "invalid pane identifier",
+        ));
+    }
+    let security = Security::pipe()?;
+    let name: Vec<u16> = format!("{PIPE_PATH_PREFIX}ivy-{id}")
+        .encode_utf16()
+        .chain(Some(0))
+        .collect();
+    let handle = new_pipe(&name, &security, true)?;
+    Ok(Listener {
+        inner: Arc::new(Mutex::new(ListenerInner {
+            name,
+            security,
+            handle: Some(handle),
+            first: false,
+        })),
+    })
+}
+
+pub struct StopSignal {
+    handle: Arc<Handle>,
+    armed: bool,
+}
+pub struct Acceptor {
+    listener: Arc<Mutex<ListenerInner>>,
+    stop: Arc<Handle>,
+    pending: Mutex<Option<(HANDLE, Box<OVERLAPPED>, Handle)>>,
+    pending_ready: AtomicBool,
+    connected: Mutex<bool>,
+}
+unsafe impl Send for Acceptor {}
+unsafe impl Sync for Acceptor {}
+
+pub fn acceptor(listener: Listener) -> io::Result<(StopSignal, Acceptor)> {
+    let stop = Arc::new(event()?);
+    let acceptor = Acceptor {
+        listener: Arc::clone(&listener.inner),
+        stop: Arc::clone(&stop),
+        pending: Mutex::new(None),
+        pending_ready: AtomicBool::new(false),
+        connected: Mutex::new(false),
+    };
+    Ok((
+        StopSignal {
+            handle: stop,
+            armed: true,
+        },
+        acceptor,
+    ))
+}
+
+fn event() -> io::Result<Handle> {
+    // SAFETY: manual reset event, initially nonsignaled, and non-inheritable.
+    Handle::new(unsafe { CreateEventW(null(), 1, 0, null()) })
+}
+
+impl Acceptor {
+    pub fn wait_for_stop(&self, timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<bool> {
+        let millis = timeout.as_millis().min((u32::MAX - 1) as u128) as u32;
+        match unsafe { WaitForSingleObject(self.stop.0, millis) } {
+            WAIT_OBJECT_0 => Ok(true),
+            WAIT_TIMEOUT => Ok(false),
+            _ => Err(winerr()),
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn wait(&self) -> io::Result<super::AcceptorReady> {
+        if *self.connected.lock().unwrap() {
+            return Ok(super::AcceptorReady::Connection);
+        }
+        let mut pending = self.pending.lock().unwrap();
+        if pending.is_none() {
+            let listener = self.listener.lock().unwrap();
+            let pipe = listener
+                .handle
+                .as_ref()
+                .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe, "listener stopped"))?;
+            let ev = event()?;
+            let mut ov: Box<OVERLAPPED> = Box::new(unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() });
+            ov.hEvent = ev.0;
+            // SAFETY: OVERLAPPED and event stay in `pending` until completion is drained.
+            let ok = unsafe { ConnectNamedPipe(pipe.0, &mut *ov) };
+            if ok == 0 {
+                let code = unsafe { GetLastError() };
+                if code == ERROR_PIPE_CONNECTED || code == ERROR_NO_DATA {
+                    *self.connected.lock().unwrap() = true;
+                    return Ok(super::AcceptorReady::Connection);
+                }
+                if code != ERROR_IO_PENDING {
+                    return Err(io::Error::from_raw_os_error(code as i32));
+                }
+            }
+            *pending = Some((pipe.0, ov, ev));
+            self.pending_ready.store(true, Ordering::Release);
+        }
+        let (_, _ov, ev) = pending.as_ref().unwrap();
+        // Stop is first so a simultaneous connect completion is treated as cancellation.
+        let handles = [self.stop.0, ev.0];
+        // SAFETY: both handles and the overlapped operation remain owned throughout the wait.
+        let result = unsafe { WaitForMultipleObjects(2, handles.as_ptr(), 0, INFINITE) };
+        if result == WAIT_OBJECT_0 {
+            let (pipe, ov, _) = pending.take().unwrap();
+            self.pending_ready.store(false, Ordering::Release);
+            unsafe {
+                CancelIoEx(pipe, &*ov);
+            }
+            let mut transferred = 0;
+            unsafe {
+                GetOverlappedResult(pipe, &*ov, &mut transferred, 1);
+            }
+            return Ok(super::AcceptorReady::Stop);
+        }
+        if result != WAIT_OBJECT_0 + 1 {
+            return Err(winerr());
+        }
+        let (pipe, ov, _) = pending.take().unwrap();
+        self.pending_ready.store(false, Ordering::Release);
+        let mut transferred = 0;
+        if unsafe { GetOverlappedResult(pipe, &*ov, &mut transferred, 0) } == 0 {
+            if unsafe { GetLastError() } == ERROR_NO_DATA {
+                *self.connected.lock().unwrap() = true;
+                return Ok(super::AcceptorReady::Connection);
+            }
+            return Err(winerr());
+        }
+        *self.connected.lock().unwrap() = true;
+        Ok(super::AcceptorReady::Connection)
+    }
+
+    pub fn accept(&self) -> io::Result<Stream> {
+        if !std::mem::replace(&mut *self.connected.lock().unwrap(), false) {
+            return Err(io::Error::new(
+                io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock,
+                "no pipe connection is ready",
+            ));
+        }
+        let mut listener = self.listener.lock().unwrap();
+        let pipe = listener
+            .handle
+            .take()
+            .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::BrokenPipe, "listener stopped"))?;
+        // Keep the pipe name owned while preparing its successor instance.
+        listener.handle = Some(new_pipe(
+            &listener.name,
+            &listener.security,
+            listener.first,
+        )?);
+        listener.first = false;
+        Ok(Stream {
+            handle: pipe,
+            stop: Arc::clone(&self.stop),
+            tracker: Arc::new(IoTracker::default()),
+            read_timeout: Mutex::new(None),
+            write_timeout: Mutex::new(None),
+        })
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for Acceptor {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        if let Ok(pending) = self.pending.get_mut() {
+            if let Some((pipe, overlapped, _event)) = pending.take() {
+                self.pending_ready.store(false, Ordering::Release);
+                let mut transferred = 0;
+                unsafe {
+                    CancelIoEx(pipe, &*overlapped);
+                    GetOverlappedResult(pipe, &*overlapped, &mut transferred, 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Drop for StopSignal {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        if self.armed {
+            unsafe {
+                SetEvent(self.handle.0);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Listener {
+    pub fn accept(&self) -> io::Result<Stream> {
+        let (mut stop, acceptor) = acceptor(self.clone())?;
+        match acceptor.wait()? {
+            super::AcceptorReady::Connection => {
+                let stream = acceptor.accept()?;
+                stop.armed = false;
+                Ok(stream)
+            }
+            super::AcceptorReady::Stop => Err(io::Error::new(
+                io::ErrorKind::Interrupted,
+                "listener stopped",
+            )),
+        }
+    }
+}
+impl Clone for Listener {
+    fn clone(&self) -> Self {
+        Self {
+            inner: Arc::clone(&self.inner),
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+pub struct Stream {
+    handle: Handle,
+    stop: Arc<Handle>,
+    tracker: Arc<IoTracker>,
+    read_timeout: Mutex<Option<Duration>>,
+    write_timeout: Mutex<Option<Duration>>,
+}
+unsafe impl Send for Stream {}
+unsafe impl Sync for Stream {}
+#[derive(Default)]
+struct IoTracker {
+    in_flight: AtomicUsize,
+    pending: AtomicUsize,
+}
+struct IoGuard(Arc<IoTracker>, bool);
+impl IoGuard {
+    fn begin(tracker: &Arc<IoTracker>) -> Self {
+        tracker.in_flight.fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
+        Self(Arc::clone(tracker), false)
+    }
+    fn mark_pending(&mut self) {
+        self.0.pending.fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
+        self.1 = true;
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for IoGuard {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        if self.1 {
+            self.0.pending.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
+        }
+        self.0.in_flight.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
+    }
+}
+impl Stream {
+    pub fn verify_server_pid(&self, expected: u32) -> io::Result<()> {
+        if expected == 0 {
+            return Err(io::Error::new(
+                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+                "expected server PID is missing",
+            ));
+        }
+        let mut actual = 0;
+        if unsafe { GetNamedPipeServerProcessId(self.handle.0, &mut actual) } == 0 {
+            return Err(winerr());
+        }
+        if actual != expected {
+            return Err(io::Error::new(
+                io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+                format!("control pipe server PID {actual} did not match expected PID {expected}"),
+            ));
+        }
+        Ok(())
+    }
+    pub fn try_clone(&self) -> io::Result<Self> {
+        let mut duplicate = null_mut();
+        // SAFETY: duplicate refers to an independently closed duplicate of the current-process handle.
+        if unsafe {
+            DuplicateHandle(
+                GetCurrentProcess(),
+                self.handle.0,
+                GetCurrentProcess(),
+                &mut duplicate,
+                0,
+                0,
+                DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS,
+            )
+        } == 0
+        {
+            return Err(winerr());
+        }
+        Ok(Self {
+            handle: Handle(duplicate),
+            stop: Arc::clone(&self.stop),
+            tracker: Arc::clone(&self.tracker),
+            read_timeout: Mutex::new(*self.read_timeout.lock().unwrap()),
+            write_timeout: Mutex::new(*self.write_timeout.lock().unwrap()),
+        })
+    }
+    pub fn set_recv_timeout(&self, timeout: Option<Duration>) -> io::Result<()> {
+        *self.read_timeout.lock().unwrap() = timeout;
+        Ok(())
+    }
+    pub fn set_send_timeout(&self, timeout: Option<Duration>) -> io::Result<()> {
+        *self.write_timeout.lock().unwrap() = timeout;
+        Ok(())
+    }
+    pub fn raw_handle(&self) -> HANDLE {
+        self.handle.0
+    }
+}
+
+fn transfer(
+    handle: HANDLE,
+    stop: HANDLE,
+    tracker: &Arc<IoTracker>,
+    buffer: *mut u8,
+    len: u32,
+    timeout: Option<Duration>,
+    write: bool,
+) -> io::Result<usize> {
+    let mut operation = IoGuard::begin(tracker);
+    let ev = event()?;
+    let mut ov: OVERLAPPED = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
+    ov.hEvent = ev.0;
+    let mut transferred = 0;
+    let ok = unsafe {
+        if write {
+            WriteFile(handle, buffer, len, &mut transferred, &mut ov)
+        } else {
+            ReadFile(handle, buffer, len, &mut transferred, &mut ov)
+        }
+    };
+    if ok != 0 {
+        return Ok(transferred as usize);
+    }
+    let code = unsafe { GetLastError() };
+    if code != ERROR_IO_PENDING {
+        if !write && code == ERROR_BROKEN_PIPE {
+            return Ok(0);
+        }
+        return Err(io::Error::from_raw_os_error(code as i32));
+    }
+    operation.mark_pending();
+    let millis = timeout
+        .map(|d| d.as_millis().min((u32::MAX - 1) as u128) as u32)
+        .unwrap_or(INFINITE);
+    let handles = [stop, ev.0];
+    let wait = unsafe { WaitForMultipleObjects(2, handles.as_ptr(), 0, millis) };
+    if wait != WAIT_OBJECT_0 + 1 {
+        unsafe {
+            CancelIoEx(handle, &ov);
+            GetOverlappedResult(handle, &ov, &mut transferred, 1);
+        }
+        return Err(if wait == WAIT_OBJECT_0 {
+            io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::Interrupted, "pane channel stopped")
+        } else if wait == WAIT_TIMEOUT {
+            io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "pipe operation timed out")
+        } else {
+            winerr()
+        });
+    }
+    if unsafe { GetOverlappedResult(handle, &ov, &mut transferred, 0) } == 0 {
+        let code = unsafe { GetLastError() };
+        if !write && code == ERROR_BROKEN_PIPE {
+            return Ok(0);
+        }
+        return Err(io::Error::from_raw_os_error(code as i32));
+    }
+    Ok(transferred as usize)
+}
+impl Read for Stream {
+    fn read(&mut self, buf: &mut [u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
+        (&*self).read(buf)
+    }
+}
+impl Write for Stream {
+    fn write(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
+        (&*self).write(buf)
+    }
+    fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
+        Ok(())
+    }
+}
+impl Read for &Stream {
+    fn read(&mut self, buf: &mut [u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
+        if buf.is_empty() {
+            return Ok(0);
+        }
+        transfer(
+            self.handle.0,
+            self.stop.0,
+            &self.tracker,
+            buf.as_mut_ptr(),
+            buf.len().min(u32::MAX as usize) as u32,
+            *self.read_timeout.lock().unwrap(),
+            false,
+        )
+    }
+}
+impl Write for &Stream {
+    fn write(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
+        if buf.is_empty() {
+            return Ok(0);
+        }
+        transfer(
+            self.handle.0,
+            self.stop.0,
+            &self.tracker,
+            buf.as_ptr() as *mut u8,
+            buf.len().min(u32::MAX as usize) as u32,
+            *self.write_timeout.lock().unwrap(),
+            true,
+        )
+    }
+    fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
+        Ok(())
+    }
+}
+
+pub fn connect(_dir: &Path, id: &str) -> io::Result<Stream> {
+    if id.is_empty() || !id.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
+        return Err(io::Error::new(
+            io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
+            "invalid pane identifier",
+        ));
+    }
+    let name = wide(OsStr::new(&format!("{PIPE_PATH_PREFIX}ivy-{id}")));
+    let handle = unsafe {
+        CreateFileW(
+            name.as_ptr(),
+            GENERIC_READ | GENERIC_WRITE,
+            0,
+            null(),
+            OPEN_EXISTING,
+            FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED,
+            null_mut(),
+        )
+    };
+    Ok(Stream {
+        handle: Handle::new(handle)?,
+        stop: Arc::new(event()?),
+        tracker: Arc::new(IoTracker::default()),
+        read_timeout: Mutex::new(None),
+        write_timeout: Mutex::new(None),
+    })
+}
+
+pub fn private_to_this_user(_dir: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+    panel_contract::windows_directory::validate(_dir).map_err(|failure| {
+        if failure.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound {
+            io::Error::new(
+                failure.kind(),
+                format!(
+                    "this console's panel could not be reached at {} (directory is gone)",
+                    _dir.display()
+                ),
+            )
+        } else if failure.kind() == io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied {
+            io::Error::new(
+                failure.kind(),
+                format!("{} is not a private directory of this user", _dir.display()),
+            )
+        } else {
+            io::Error::new(
+                failure.kind(),
+                format!(
+                    "this console's panel could not be reached at {} ({failure})",
+                    _dir.display()
+                ),
+            )
+        }
+    })
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    use super::*;
+    use std::io::{Read, Write};
+    use std::thread;
+    use std::time::Instant;
+
+    #[test]
+    fn first_instance_refuses_a_duplicate_and_pipe_dacl_is_exact() {
+        let panel = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let listener = bind(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        assert!(
+            bind(panel.path(), panel.id()).is_err(),
+            "a second first instance captured the pipe name"
+        );
+        assert_pipe_acl(&listener);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn a_client_that_connects_before_the_accept_wait_is_served() {
+        let panel = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let listener = bind(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        let mut client = connect(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        let mut server = listener.accept().unwrap();
+        client.write_all(b"x").unwrap();
+        let mut byte = [0];
+        assert_eq!(server.read(&mut byte).unwrap(), 1);
+        assert_eq!(byte[0], b'x');
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn pane_ids_bind_distinct_pipe_names() {
+        let first = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let second = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let first_listener = bind(first.path(), first.id()).unwrap();
+        let second_listener = bind(second.path(), second.id()).unwrap();
+        let mut first_client = connect(first.path(), first.id()).unwrap();
+        let mut second_client = connect(second.path(), second.id()).unwrap();
+        let mut first_server = first_listener.accept().unwrap();
+        let mut second_server = second_listener.accept().unwrap();
+        first_client.write_all(b"a").unwrap();
+        second_client.write_all(b"b").unwrap();
+        let mut byte = [0];
+        assert_eq!(first_server.read(&mut byte).unwrap(), 1);
+        assert_eq!(byte[0], b'a');
+        assert_eq!(second_server.read(&mut byte).unwrap(), 1);
+        assert_eq!(byte[0], b'b');
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn dropping_a_channel_cancels_and_drains_a_pending_accept() {
+        let panel = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let listener = bind(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        let (stop, acceptor) = acceptor(listener).unwrap();
+        let acceptor = Arc::new(acceptor);
+        let waiting = Arc::clone(&acceptor);
+        let worker = thread::spawn(move || waiting.wait());
+        wait_for_accept_pending(&acceptor);
+        drop(stop);
+        assert!(matches!(
+            worker.join().unwrap().unwrap(),
+            super::super::AcceptorReady::Stop
+        ));
+        assert!(
+            acceptor.pending.lock().unwrap().is_none(),
+            "pending connect OVERLAPPED was retained after stop"
+        );
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn stop_cancels_idle_and_partial_reads_after_completion_is_drained() {
+        for partial in [false, true] {
+            let panel = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+            let listener = bind(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+            let (stop, acceptor) = acceptor(listener).unwrap();
+            let mut client = connect(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+            acceptor.wait().unwrap();
+            let mut server = acceptor.accept().unwrap();
+            if partial {
+                client.write_all(b"x").unwrap();
+            }
+            let tracker = Arc::clone(&server.tracker);
+            let worker = thread::spawn(move || {
+                let mut bytes = [0; 2];
+                server.read_exact(&mut bytes)
+            });
+            wait_for_io_pending(&tracker);
+            drop(stop);
+            let result = worker.join().unwrap();
+            assert_eq!(result.unwrap_err().kind(), io::ErrorKind::Interrupted);
+            assert_eq!(tracker.pending.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+            assert_eq!(tracker.in_flight.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn stop_cancels_a_write_to_a_peer_that_is_not_reading() {
+        let panel = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let listener = bind(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        let (stop, acceptor) = acceptor(listener).unwrap();
+        let _client = connect(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        acceptor.wait().unwrap();
+        let mut server = acceptor.accept().unwrap();
+        let tracker = Arc::clone(&server.tracker);
+        let worker = thread::spawn(move || server.write_all(&vec![b'x'; 16 * 1024 * 1024]));
+        wait_for_io_pending(&tracker);
+        drop(stop);
+        let result = worker.join().unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(result.unwrap_err().kind(), io::ErrorKind::Interrupted);
+        assert_eq!(tracker.pending.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+        assert_eq!(tracker.in_flight.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn connect_racing_with_stop_leaves_no_accept_operation_pending() {
+        let panel = panel_contract::PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let listener = bind(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        let (stop, acceptor) = acceptor(listener).unwrap();
+        let acceptor = Arc::new(acceptor);
+        let waiting = Arc::clone(&acceptor);
+        let worker = thread::spawn(move || waiting.wait());
+        wait_for_accept_pending(&acceptor);
+        let client = connect(panel.path(), panel.id()).unwrap();
+        drop(stop);
+        let outcome = worker.join().unwrap().unwrap();
+        if matches!(outcome, super::super::AcceptorReady::Connection) {
+            drop(acceptor.accept().unwrap());
+        }
+        drop(client);
+        assert!(
+            acceptor.pending.lock().unwrap().is_none(),
+            "connect/stop race left a live OVERLAPPED"
+        );
+    }
+
+    fn wait_for_accept_pending(acceptor: &Acceptor) {
+        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
+        while !acceptor.pending_ready.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
+            assert!(
+                Instant::now() < deadline,
+                "accept operation never reached pending state"
+            );
+            thread::yield_now();
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn wait_for_io_pending(tracker: &IoTracker) {
+        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
+        while tracker.pending.load(Ordering::Acquire) == 0 {
+            assert!(
+                Instant::now() < deadline,
+                "I/O operation never reached pending state"
+            );
+            thread::yield_now();
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn assert_pipe_acl(listener: &Listener) {
+        let listener = listener.inner.lock().unwrap();
+        let handle = listener.handle.as_ref().unwrap().0;
+        let mut dacl = null_mut();
+        let mut descriptor = null_mut();
+        let result = unsafe {
+            GetSecurityInfo(
+                handle,
+                SE_KERNEL_OBJECT,
+                DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION,
+                null_mut(),
+                null_mut(),
+                &mut dacl,
+                null_mut(),
+                &mut descriptor,
+            )
+        };
+        assert_eq!(result, ERROR_SUCCESS);
+        let descriptor = Security(descriptor);
+        let mut control = 0;
+        let mut revision = 0;
+        assert_ne!(
+            unsafe { GetSecurityDescriptorControl(descriptor.0, &mut control, &mut revision) },
+            0
+        );
+        assert_ne!(control & SE_DACL_PROTECTED, 0);
+        let mut info = ACL_SIZE_INFORMATION {
+            AceCount: 0,
+            AclBytesInUse: 0,
+            AclBytesFree: 0,
+        };
+        assert_ne!(
+            unsafe {
+                GetAclInformation(
+                    dacl,
+                    (&mut info as *mut ACL_SIZE_INFORMATION).cast(),
+                    std::mem::size_of::<ACL_SIZE_INFORMATION>() as u32,
+                    AclSizeInformation,
+                )
+            },
+            0
+        );
+        let sid_text = current_sid_string().unwrap();
+        let sid_wide: Vec<u16> = sid_text.encode_utf16().chain(Some(0)).collect();
+        let mut user_sid = null_mut();
+        assert_ne!(
+            unsafe { ConvertStringSidToSidW(sid_wide.as_ptr(), &mut user_sid) },
+            0
+        );
+        let user_sid = Security(user_sid);
+        let mut system_sid = [0x00000101u32, 0x05000000, 18];
+        let system_sid = system_sid.as_mut_ptr().cast::<std::ffi::c_void>();
+        let system_is_user = unsafe { EqualSid(user_sid.0, system_sid) } != 0;
+        assert_eq!(info.AceCount, if system_is_user { 1 } else { 2 });
+        let mut user_found = false;
+        let mut system_found = false;
+        for i in 0..info.AceCount {
+            let mut ace = null_mut();
+            assert_ne!(unsafe { GetAce(dacl, i, &mut ace) }, 0);
+            let header = unsafe { &*(ace.cast::<ACE_HEADER>()) };
+            let allow = unsafe { &*(ace.cast::<ACCESS_ALLOWED_ACE>()) };
+            assert_eq!(header.AceType, 0);
+            assert_eq!(header.AceFlags, 0);
+            assert_eq!(allow.Mask, FILE_ALL_ACCESS);
+            let ace_sid = (&allow.SidStart as *const u32)
+                .cast_mut()
+                .cast::<std::ffi::c_void>();
+            if unsafe { EqualSid(ace_sid, user_sid.0) } != 0 {
+                user_found = true;
+            } else if unsafe { EqualSid(ace_sid, system_sid) } != 0 {
+                system_found = true;
+            } else {
+                panic!("pipe DACL contains an unexpected principal");
+            }
+        }
+        assert!(user_found);
+        assert!(system_is_user || system_found);
+    }
+}

+    /// Starts a callback-free Windows channel backed by bounded snapshots and a native UI wake.
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    pub fn open_windows(
+        dir: &Path,

+
+    /// Stops accepting and joins this channel's owned workers before callers bank final metrics.
+    /// Safe to call more than once; `Drop` performs the same shutdown if omitted.
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    pub fn shutdown(&mut self) {
+        self.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
+        if let Some(services) = self.windows_services.take() {
+            services.revoke();
+        }
+        drop(self.stopper.take());
+        if let Some(acceptor) = self.acceptor.take() {
+            let _ = acceptor.join();
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(all(test, windows))]
+    fn open_windows_with_test_hooks(
+        dir: &Path,
+        id: &str,
+        to_the_window: SyncSender<Delivery>,
+        windows: crate::windows_services::WindowsServices,

+    ) -> io::Result<Self> {

+    }
+
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    fn open_windows_inner(
+        dir: &Path,
+        id: &str,
+        to_the_window: SyncSender<Delivery>,
+        windows: crate::windows_services::WindowsServices,
+        test_hooks: Option<Arc<TestHooks>>,
+    ) -> io::Result<Self> {
+        let listener = endpoint::bind(dir, id)?;
+        let (stopper, acceptor) = endpoint::acceptor(listener)?;
+        let stop = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
+        let alive = Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(1));
+        let workers = Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
+        let document = windows.document.clone();
+        let plan = windows.plan.clone();
+        let wake_capability = windows.wake.clone();
+        let services = Services {
+            reader: None,
+            session: None,
+            publisher: None,
+            inspector: None,
+            connection_workers: Some(Arc::clone(&workers)),
+            releases: Some(windows.release_registry()),
+            document_snapshot: Some(document),
+            plan_snapshot: Some(plan),
+            test_hooks,
+        };
+        let acceptor_thread = std::thread::Builder::new()
+            .name(format!("panel-control {id}"))
+            .spawn({
+                let stop = Arc::clone(&stop);
+                let alive = Arc::clone(&alive);
+                let id = id.to_owned();
+                let wake_id = id.clone();
+                let wake: Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync> = Arc::new(move || {
+                    if let Err(problem) = wake_capability.post() {
+                        eprintln!(
+                            "console {wake_id}: could not wake the control window ({problem})"
+                        );
+                    }
+                });
+                let worker_registry = Arc::clone(&workers);
+                move || {
+                    let result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
+                        accept_until_stopped(
+                            &id,
+                            &acceptor,
+                            &stop,
+                            &alive,
+                            &to_the_window,
+                            &services,
+                            &wake,
+                        )

+struct AliveGuard(Arc<AtomicUsize>);
+impl Drop for AliveGuard {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {

+#[cfg(windows)]
+impl Drop for WorkerCloseGuard {

+#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
+mod windows_lifecycle_tests {
+    use super::*;
+    use panel_contract::PanelDir;
+    use std::sync::mpsc::Receiver;
+    use std::time::Instant;
+
+    static TEST_LOCK: std::sync::Mutex<()> = std::sync::Mutex::new(());
+
+    fn connect(dir: &Path, id: &str) -> io::Result<Stream> {
+        endpoint::connect_authenticated(dir, id, std::process::id())
+    }
+
+    struct Pane {
+        dir: PanelDir,
+        channel: Option<Channel>,
+        inbox: Receiver<Delivery>,
+        sender: SyncSender<Delivery>,
+        releases: crate::windows_services::ReleaseRegistry,
+        document: crate::windows_services::DocumentReader,
+        _window: crate::windows_services::tests::TestWindow,
+    }
+
+    fn pane(capacity: usize) -> Pane {
+        pane_with_hooks(capacity, None)
+    }
+
+    fn pane_with_hooks(capacity: usize, hooks: Option<Arc<TestHooks>>) -> Pane {
+        let dir = PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let (sender, inbox) = sync_channel(capacity);
+        let window = crate::windows_services::tests::test_message_window();
+        let (services, _document, _plan) =
+            crate::windows_services::WindowsServices::new(window.hwnd()).unwrap();
+        let releases = services.release_registry();
+        let document = services.document.clone();
+        let sender_copy = sender.clone();
+        let channel = match hooks {
+            Some(hooks) => {
+                Channel::open_windows_with_test_hooks(dir.path(), dir.id(), sender, services, hooks)
+                    .unwrap()
+            }
+            None => Channel::open_windows(dir.path(), dir.id(), sender, services).unwrap(),
+        };
+        Pane {
+            dir,
+            channel: Some(channel),
+            inbox,
+            sender: sender_copy,
+            releases,
+            document,
+            _window: window,
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn request(stream: &mut Stream, request: &str) {
+        stream.write_all(request.as_bytes()).unwrap();
+    }
+
+    fn line(stream: &mut Stream) -> String {
+        let mut bytes = Vec::new();
+        let mut byte = [0u8; 1];
+        loop {
+            match stream.read(&mut byte) {
+                Ok(0) => break,
+                Ok(_) if byte[0] == b'\n' => break,
+                Ok(_) => bytes.push(byte[0]),
+                Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock => std::thread::yield_now(),
+                Err(error) => panic!("read response failed: {error}"),
+            }
+        }
+        String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap()
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn held_snapshot_returns_busy_and_drop_joins_idle_worker() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let mut pane = pane(4);
+        let _held = pane.document.lock_for_test();
+        let mut client = connect(pane.dir.path(), pane.dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(&mut client, "{\"tool\":\"panel_get\"}\n");
+        let reply = line(&mut client);
+        assert!(reply.contains("snapshot is busy"), "{reply}");
+        let alive = Arc::clone(&pane.channel.as_ref().unwrap().alive);
+        let started = Instant::now();
+        drop(pane.channel.take());
+        assert!(started.elapsed() < ANSWER_DEADLINE + crate::wire::REPLY_DEADLINE + RECV_TIMEOUT);
+        assert_eq!(
+            alive.load(Ordering::Acquire),
+            0,
+            "drop returned with a worker alive"
+        );
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn full_inbox_keeps_close_pending_until_delivery_completes() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let mut pane = pane(1);
+        let mut client = connect(pane.dir.path(), pane.dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(
+            &mut client,
+            "{\"tool\":\"panel_set\",\"source\":\"test\",\"document\":{\"lines\":[]}}\n",
+        );
+        let mut delivery = pane.inbox.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap();
+        let id = delivery.connection;
+        delivery.answer.send(Answer::Taken).unwrap();
+        let _ = line(&mut client);
+        let (answer, _) = sync_channel(1);
+        pane.sender
+            .try_send(Delivery {
+                connection: u64::MAX,
+                what: What::Closed,
+                origin: PublicationOrigin::Unknown,
+                answer,
+                release: None,
+            })
+            .expect("keep the app inbox full during disconnect");
+        drop(client);
+        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
+        while Instant::now() < deadline {
+            if pane.releases.is_closed_pending(id) {
+                break;
+            }
+            std::thread::yield_now();
+        }
+        assert!(
+            pane.releases.is_closed_pending(id),
+            "worker did not record close"
+        );
+        assert!(
+            pane.releases.take_closed_ready().is_empty(),
+            "close overtook admitted work"
+        );
+        delivery.complete();
+        assert_eq!(pane.releases.take_closed_ready(), vec![id]);
+        drop(pane.channel.take());
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn drop_joins_worker_while_ui_queue_is_not_dispatched() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let mut pane = pane(1);
+        let alive = Arc::clone(&pane.channel.as_ref().unwrap().alive);
+        let mut client = connect(pane.dir.path(), pane.dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(
+            &mut client,
+            "{\"tool\":\"panel_set\",\"source\":\"test\",\"document\":{\"lines\":[]}}\n",
+        );
+        let delivery = pane.inbox.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap();
+        // Leave the delivery unanswered, matching a UI thread stopped in native dispatch. The
+        // finite answer deadline is part of the specified drop envelope.
+        let started = Instant::now();
+        drop(pane.channel.take());
+        assert!(started.elapsed() <= ANSWER_DEADLINE + crate::wire::REPLY_DEADLINE + RECV_TIMEOUT);
+        assert_eq!(alive.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+        drop(delivery);
+        drop(client);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn committed_document_and_plan_read_without_ui_dispatch() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let dir = PanelDir::create().unwrap();
+        let (sender, inbox) = sync_channel(1);
+        let window = crate::windows_services::tests::test_message_window();
+        let (services, document, plan) =
+            crate::windows_services::WindowsServices::new(window.hwnd()).unwrap();
+        document
+            .publish(r#"{"lines":["committed"]}"#.into())
+            .unwrap();
+        plan.publish_document(r#"{"plans":[]}"#.into()).unwrap();
+        let channel = Channel::open_windows(dir.path(), dir.id(), sender, services).unwrap();
+        let mut client = connect(dir.path(), dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(&mut client, "{\"tool\":\"panel_get\"}\n");
+        assert!(line(&mut client).contains("committed"));
+        request(&mut client, "{\"tool\":\"plan_get\"}\n");
+        assert!(line(&mut client).contains("plans"));
+        assert!(
+            inbox.try_recv().is_err(),
+            "snapshot reads entered the UI inbox"
+        );
+        drop(channel);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn timed_out_split_delivery_stays_pending_until_late_ui_completion() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let mut pane = pane(4);
+        let mut client = connect(pane.dir.path(), pane.dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(
+            &mut client,
+            "{\"tool\":\"split_open\",\"side\":\"right\"}\n",
+        );
+        let delivery = pane.inbox.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap();
+        assert!(matches!(delivery.what, What::Open { .. }));
+        let id = delivery.connection;
+        let mut deferred = vec![delivery];
+        let timeout_reply = line(&mut client);
+        assert!(timeout_reply.contains("five seconds"), "{timeout_reply}");
+        request(&mut client, "{\"tool\":\"split_read\",\"pane\":\"p\"}\n");
+        assert!(line(&mut client).contains("not draining"));
+        drop(client);
+        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
+        while Instant::now() < deadline && !pane.releases.is_closed_pending(id) {
+            std::thread::yield_now();
+        }
+        assert!(pane.releases.take_closed_ready().is_empty());
+        deferred.pop().unwrap().complete();
+        assert_eq!(pane.releases.take_closed_ready(), vec![id]);
+        drop(pane.channel.take());
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn stop_racing_connections_returns_with_no_registered_workers() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let mut pane = pane(8);
+        let alive = Arc::clone(&pane.channel.as_ref().unwrap().alive);
+        let start = Arc::new(std::sync::Barrier::new(5));
+        let mut connectors = Vec::new();
+        for _ in 0..4 {
+            let dir = pane.dir.path().to_owned();
+            let id = pane.dir.id().to_owned();
+            let start = Arc::clone(&start);
+            connectors.push(std::thread::spawn(move || {
+                let mut stream = connect(&dir, &id).ok();
+                start.wait();
+                if let Some(stream) = stream.as_mut() {
+                    let _ = stream.write_all(b"{\"tool\":\"panel_get\"}");
+                }
+            }));
+        }
+        start.wait();
+        drop(pane.channel.take());
+        for connector in connectors {
+            connector.join().unwrap();
+        }
+        assert_eq!(alive.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn stop_waits_for_worker_registration_in_progress() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let hooks = Arc::new(TestHooks::default());
+        let gate = Arc::new(std::sync::Barrier::new(2));
+        *hooks.registration_gate.lock().unwrap() = Some(Arc::clone(&gate));
+        let mut pane = pane_with_hooks(2, Some(hooks.clone()));
+        let alive = Arc::clone(&pane.channel.as_ref().unwrap().alive);
+        let mut client = connect(pane.dir.path(), pane.dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(&mut client, "{\"tool\":\"panel_get\"}\n");
+        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
+        while !hooks.registration_entered.load(Ordering::Acquire) && Instant::now() < deadline {
+            std::thread::yield_now();
+        }
+        assert!(hooks.registration_entered.load(Ordering::Acquire));
+        let channel = pane.channel.take().unwrap();
+        let dropping = std::thread::spawn(move || drop(channel));
+        // The acceptor is held before its worker handle enters the owned registry. Release it only
+        // after Drop has started, proving shutdown joins the in-flight registration path.
+        gate.wait();
+        dropping.join().unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(alive.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn panic_and_spawn_failure_paths_still_join_and_close_slots() {
+        let _serial = TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let acceptor_hooks = Arc::new(TestHooks::default());
+        acceptor_hooks.panic_acceptor.store(true, Ordering::Release);
+        let mut panicked_acceptor = pane_with_hooks(1, Some(Arc::clone(&acceptor_hooks)));
+        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
+        while !acceptor_hooks.acceptor_panicked.load(Ordering::Acquire) && Instant::now() < deadline
+        {
+            std::thread::yield_now();
+        }
+        assert!(acceptor_hooks.acceptor_panicked.load(Ordering::Acquire));
+        let acceptor_alive = Arc::clone(&panicked_acceptor.channel.as_ref().unwrap().alive);
+        drop(panicked_acceptor.channel.take());
+        assert_eq!(acceptor_alive.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+
+        let spawn_hooks = Arc::new(TestHooks::default());
+        spawn_hooks.fail_worker_spawn.store(true, Ordering::Release);
+        let mut spawn_failed = pane_with_hooks(1, Some(spawn_hooks));
+        let mut client = connect(spawn_failed.dir.path(), spawn_failed.dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(&mut client, "{\"tool\":\"panel_get\"}\n");
+        assert!(line(&mut client).contains("could not serve the connection"));
+        let spawned_alive = Arc::clone(&spawn_failed.channel.as_ref().unwrap().alive);
+        drop(spawn_failed.channel.take());
+        assert_eq!(spawned_alive.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+
+        let worker_hooks = Arc::new(TestHooks::default());
+        worker_hooks.panic_worker.store(true, Ordering::Release);
+        let mut panicked_worker = pane_with_hooks(1, Some(Arc::clone(&worker_hooks)));
+        let worker_alive = Arc::clone(&panicked_worker.channel.as_ref().unwrap().alive);
+        let mut client = connect(panicked_worker.dir.path(), panicked_worker.dir.id()).unwrap();
+        request(&mut client, "{\"tool\":\"panel_get\"}\n");
+        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
+        while !worker_hooks.worker_panicked.load(Ordering::Acquire) && Instant::now() < deadline {
+            std::thread::yield_now();
+        }
+        assert!(worker_hooks.worker_panicked.load(Ordering::Acquire));
+        drop(panicked_worker.channel.take());
+        assert_eq!(worker_alive.load(Ordering::Acquire), 0);
+    }
+}
