### crates/app/src/control/applying.rs

```
//! Apply pane-scoped deliveries without changing panel ownership for agent metadata.
use super::*;
impl PaneControl {
    /// Applies everything waiting, and answers whoever is waiting for an answer.
    ///
    /// **The queue is bounded at [`MAX_PENDING`], and the earlier shape that left it unbounded was
    /// wrong for a reason review found.** The argument for leaving it open was that a pane serves a
    /// fixed, small number of connections and each one blocks on its own reply, so what is ordinarily
    /// waiting is one request per connection. What that missed is a caller that *gave up*: its thread
    /// goes back to reading with its delivery still queued, and a window that is minimised runs no
    /// pass to drain it — so a producer parked against it piled up one document per connection per
    /// timeout, unbounded. With `sync_channel` the send applies backpressure (or the listener refuses,
    /// see its `publish`), and memory stays capped.
    ///
    /// **A caller that gave up still gets its document applied when a pass runs**, and the wording on
    /// the other side is what makes that honest: the reply a timed-out caller reads says the window
    /// did not answer in time, never that nothing was published.
    pub fn apply(
        &mut self,
        state: &mut PanelState,
        signals: &mut crate::agent_signals::Signals,
        now: SystemTime,
    ) {
        while let Ok(mut delivery) = self.inbox.try_recv() {
            let what = std::mem::replace(&mut delivery.what, What::Closed);
            match what {
                What::Overlay {
                    panel,
                    holder,
                    incarnation,
                    authority,
                } => {
                    if state.accepts_overlay(holder, incarnation)
                        && authority.current()
                        && state.apply_overlay(panel, now).is_ok()
                    {
                        self.did.overlaid += 1;
                    } else {
                        self.did.overlays_refused += 1;
                        crate::panel::overlay::refused_at_apply(state);
                    }
                    #[cfg(windows)]
                    delivery.complete();
                }

                What::PublishAttested {
                    source,
                    panel,
                    peer,
                } => {
                    let answer = match crate::panel::provenance::deliver_observed(
                        state,
                        delivery.connection,
                        &source,
                        panel,
                        now,
                        delivery.origin,
                    ) {
                        Ok(()) => {
                            if let Some(authority) = &self.authority {
                                authority.capture(state, delivery.connection, &peer);
                            }
                            self.did.applied += 1;
                            Answer::Taken
                        }
                        Err(why) => {
                            self.did.refused += 1;
                            Answer::Refused(why)
                        }
                    };
                    #[cfg(windows)]
                    delivery.complete();
                    let _ = delivery.answer.send(answer);
                }

                What::AgentSignal(snapshot) => {
                    let answer = signals
                        .receive(delivery.connection, snapshot, std::time::Instant::now())
                        .map_or_else(Answer::Refused, |_| Answer::Taken);
                    #[cfg(windows)]
                    delivery.complete();
                    let _ = delivery.answer.send(answer);
                }
                What::Publish { source, panel } => {
                    let answer = match crate::panel::provenance::deliver_observed(
                        state,
                        delivery.connection,
                        &source,
                        panel,
                        now,
                        delivery.origin,
                    ) {
                        Ok(()) => {
                            self.did.applied += 1;
                            Answer::Taken
                        }
                        Err(why) => {
                            self.did.refused += 1;
                            Answer::Refused(why)
                        }
                    };
                    #[cfg(windows)]
                    delivery.complete();
                    // The send cannot block — the answer channel takes one value — and it fails only
                    // when the caller is already gone, which changes nothing about what is on screen.
                    let _ = delivery.answer.send(answer);
                }
                What::Get => {
                    let answer = match state.document() {
                        Ok(document) => {
                            self.reads.succeeded(document.len());
                            Answer::Document(document)
                        }
                        Err(why) => {
                            self.reads.refused();
                            Answer::Refused(why)
                        }
                    };
                    #[cfg(windows)]
                    delivery.complete();
                    let _ = delivery.answer.send(answer);
                }
                // **The patch lands beside the publication and not beside the reads**, because it
                // is a write: it needs the hold, it stamps the date, and what it leaves behind is
                // what the next painting draws.
                What::Patch { ops } => {
                    let answer = match state.patch(delivery.connection, &ops, now) {
                        Ok(written) => {
                            self.did.patched += 1;
                            // **The size of what the patch left behind, not of the ops that
                            // arrived**, and it comes back from the ceiling check rather than from a
                            // second write: the document a following read would hand over is the one
                            // whose size explains this path's cost.
                            self.did.largest_serialized = self.did.largest_serialized.max(written);
                            Answer::Taken
                        }
                        Err(why) => {
                            self.did.patches_refused += 1;
                            Answer::Refused(why)
                        }
                    };
                    #[cfg(windows)]
                    delivery.complete();
                    let _ = delivery.answer.send(answer);
                }
                What::Closed => {
                    signals.closed(delivery.connection, std::time::Instant::now());
                    self.apply_closed(state, delivery);
                }
                // **The three `split_*` verbs are the window's, and a pane has no tree.** They are
                // held here until the window's drain takes them, which is where the handles, the
                // focus and the revocation live — and the window is woken for it, because the drain
                // of this very pass has already run: without that, a request arriving while the
                // window is at rest would be answered by no pass at all.
                deferred @ What::Retire => {
                    delivery.what = deferred;
                    self.defer_retirement(delivery);
                    break;
                }
                deferred @ (What::Open { .. }
                | What::Read { .. }
                | What::Close { .. }
                | What::PlanSet { .. }
                | What::PlanGet
                | What::PlanRetire) => {
                    delivery.what = deferred;
                    self.waiting_on_the_window.push(delivery);
                    (self.wake)();
                }
            }
        }
        #[cfg(windows)]
        self.apply_ready_windows_closes(state, signals);
    }

    #[cfg(windows)]
    pub(crate) fn apply_ready_windows_closes(
        &mut self,
        state: &mut PanelState,
        signals: &mut crate::agent_signals::Signals,
    ) {
        for connection in self.windows_releases.take_closed_ready() {
            signals.closed(connection, std::time::Instant::now());
            let (answer, _) = sync_channel(1);
            self.apply_closed(
                state,
                Delivery {
                    connection,
                    what: What::Closed,
                    origin: panel_control::PublicationOrigin::Unknown,
                    answer,
                    release: None,
                },
            );
        }
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/control/channel.rs

```
//! Optional off-frame services share the pane endpoint without changing panel ownership.
use super::*;

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) fn open_with_sessions(
    dir: &PanelDir,
    reader: crate::panel::DocumentReader,
    reads: ReadActivity,
    sessions: Option<panel_control::session::SessionHandler>,
    wake: impl Fn() + Send + Sync + 'static,
) -> Option<PaneControl> {
    open_services(dir, reader, reads, sessions, None, None, wake, None)
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)] // UI capabilities are assembled at this one boundary.
pub(crate) fn open_with_authority(
    dir: &PanelDir,
    state: &PanelState,
    reads: ReadActivity,
    sessions: Option<panel_control::session::SessionHandler>,
    authority: Option<Arc<crate::panel::overlay::Authority>>,
    inspector: panel_control::PublicationInspector,
    wake: impl Fn() + Send + Sync + 'static,
    hwnd: Option<isize>,
) -> Option<PaneControl> {
    open_services(
        dir,
        state.reader(),
        reads,
        sessions,
        authority.map(|authority| (state, authority)),
        Some(inspector),
        wake,
        hwnd,
    )
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)] // Mirrors the optional platform capabilities above.
fn open_services(
    dir: &PanelDir,
    reader: crate::panel::DocumentReader,
    reads: ReadActivity,
    sessions: Option<panel_control::session::SessionHandler>,
    authorization: Option<(&PanelState, Arc<crate::panel::overlay::Authority>)>,
    inspector: Option<panel_control::PublicationInspector>,
    wake: impl Fn() + Send + Sync + 'static,
    hwnd: Option<isize>,
) -> Option<PaneControl> {
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    let _ = hwnd;
    let (sender, inbox) = sync_channel(MAX_PENDING);
    #[cfg(test)]
    let test_sender = sender.clone();
    let wake: Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync> = Arc::new(wake);
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    let read_document = measured_reader(reader, reads.clone());
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    let (publisher, authority) = authorization.map_or((None, None), |(state, authority)| {
        let handler = crate::panel::overlay::service(
            state,
            Arc::clone(&authority),
            sender.clone(),
            Arc::clone(&wake),
        );
        (Some(handler), Some(authority))
    });
    #[cfg(windows)]
    let (opened, windows_publishers) = {
        let _ = (reader, sessions, authorization, inspector);
        let opened = hwnd
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                std::io::Error::new(
                    std::io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
                    "the native control window handle is unavailable",
                )
            })
            .and_then(|hwnd| panel_control::WindowsServices::new(hwnd))
            .and_then(|(services, document, plan)| {
                let releases = services.release_registry();
                Channel::open_windows(dir.path(), dir.id(), sender, services)
                    .map(|channel| (channel, document, plan, releases))
            });
        match opened {
            Ok((channel, document, plan, releases)) => {
                (Ok(channel), Some((document, plan, releases)))
            }
            Err(problem) => (Err(problem), None),
        }
    };
    #[cfg(windows)]
    let authority = None;
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    let opened = match inspector {
        Some(inspector) => Channel::open_with_publication_inspector(
            dir.path(),
            dir.id(),
            sender,
            Some(read_document),
            sessions,
            publisher,
            Some(inspector),
            Arc::clone(&wake),
        ),
        None => Channel::open_with_authorization_services(
            dir.path(),
            dir.id(),
            sender,
            Some(read_document),
            sessions,
            publisher,
            Arc::clone(&wake),
        ),
    };
    match opened {
        Ok(channel) => {
            #[cfg(not(windows))]
            channel.set_plan_reader(Arc::new(|| {
                panel_control::Reply::Failed("this console's project is not known yet".into())
            }));
            #[cfg(windows)]
            let (windows_document, windows_plan, windows_releases) = windows_publishers
                .expect("Windows channel creation retains its concrete publishers");
            Some(PaneControl {
                authority,
                plan_identity: None,
                _channel: channel,
                inbox,
                #[cfg(test)]
                test_sender,
                wake,
                waiting_on_the_window: Vec::new(),
                did: Control::default(),
                reads,
                #[cfg(windows)]
                windows_document,
                #[cfg(windows)]
                windows_plan,
                #[cfg(windows)]
                windows_releases,
            })
        }
        // **Binding nothing on a platform that cannot assert who may connect is policy, not a
        // fault, so it is not reported.** Said once per pane at every start it reads as a broken
        // terminal, and the person reading it has nothing to do about it: the platform gets its
        // channel when F2.7 can put a permission on the endpoint.
        Err(failure) if failure.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::Unsupported => None,
        Err(failure) => {
            eprintln!(
                "console {}: it goes without a control channel, its endpoint could not be bound ({failure})",
                dir.id()
            );
            None
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(windows)]
impl Drop for PaneControl {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.windows_document.revoke();
        self.windows_plan.revoke();
        self.windows_releases.revoke();
    }
}

impl PaneControl {
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    pub(crate) fn install_plan_reader(&self, reader: panel_control::listener::ReadPlan) {
        self._channel.set_plan_reader(reader);
    }
}
```

### crates/app/src/launch.rs

```
//! A console reserves its place immediately; slow Linux scope setup finishes off the frame thread.
use std::ffi::OsStr;
#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::OnceLock;
use std::sync::mpsc::{Receiver, Sender, TryRecvError, channel};
use term_core::{Backlog, Ending, ForegroundScope, Pty, Size};

static LAUNCHER: OnceLock<PathBuf> = OnceLock::new();
type Spawned = (Pty, Receiver<Vec<u8>>);

struct Ready {
    spawned: Spawned,
    startup: Vec<u8>,
}

#[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", test))]
fn prepare(spawned: Spawned) -> Ready {
    let mut startup = Vec::new();
    // term-core holds at most eight 8192-byte reads. Capture that finite FIFO prefix on the
    // worker, never drain a growing producer or copy its prefix on the drawing thread.
    for chunk in spawned.1.try_iter().take(8) {
        startup.extend(chunk);
    }
    Ready { spawned, startup }
}

#[cfg(test)]
thread_local! {
    static TEST_LAUNCHER: std::cell::RefCell<Option<PathBuf>> = const { std::cell::RefCell::new(None) };
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn launcher() -> Option<PathBuf> {
    #[cfg(test)]
    if let Some(path) = TEST_LAUNCHER.with(|launcher| launcher.borrow().clone()) {
        return Some(path);
    }
    LAUNCHER.get().cloned()
}

/// Explicit opt-in by the Ivy executable, never by a library consumer's test runner.
pub fn enable(launcher: PathBuf) {
    let _ = LAUNCHER.set(launcher);
}

pub(crate) struct Process {
    live: Option<Pty>,
    terminal: term_core::Termination,
    cost: std::time::Duration,
    pub(crate) context: Option<eframe::egui::Context>,
    pending: Option<Pending>,
    input: Vec<u8>,
    startup: usize,
    input_since: Option<std::time::Instant>,
    refused: bool,
    refusals: u64,
    pub(crate) failure: Option<String>,
    delivery_failed: bool,
    pub(crate) dismissed: bool,
}

struct Pending {
    result: Receiver<term_core::Result<Ready>>,
    _output_open: Sender<Vec<u8>>,
    reads: crate::control::ReadActivity,
}

impl From<Pty> for Process {
    fn from(live: Pty) -> Self {
        Self {
            live: Some(live),
            terminal: term_core::Termination::Pending,
            cost: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
            context: None,
            pending: None,
            input: Vec::new(),
            startup: 0,
            input_since: None,
            refused: false,
            refusals: 0,
            failure: None,
            delivery_failed: false,
            dismissed: false,
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Request<'a> {
    pub program: &'a str,
    pub args: &'a [&'a str],
    pub size: Size,
    pub env: &'a [(&'a str, Option<&'a OsStr>)],
    pub directory: Option<&'a Path>,
    pub fallback: bool,
}

impl Process {
    pub(crate) fn start(
        wake: std::sync::Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>,
        reads: crate::control::ReadActivity,
        request: Request<'_>,
    ) -> term_core::Result<(Self, Receiver<Vec<u8>>)> {
        #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
        if let Some(launcher) = launcher() {
            let program = request.program.to_owned();
            let args: Vec<String> = request.args.iter().map(|arg| (*arg).to_owned()).collect();
            let env: Vec<(String, Option<OsString>)> = request
                .env
                .iter()
                .map(|(key, value)| ((*key).to_owned(), value.map(OsStr::to_owned)))
                .collect();
            let directory = request.directory.map(Path::to_owned);
            let size = request.size;
            let fallback = request.fallback;
            return start_worker(wake, reads, move || {
                let args: Vec<&str> = args.iter().map(String::as_str).collect();
                let borrowed: Vec<(&str, Option<&OsStr>)> = env
                    .iter()
                    .map(|(key, value)| (key.as_str(), value.as_deref()))
                    .collect();
                let identity = env
                    .iter()
                    .find(|(key, _)| key == "PANEL_ID")
                    .and_then(|(_, value)| value.as_ref())
                    .map(|v| v.to_string_lossy())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "console".into());
                let spawn = |program, args: &[&str]| {
                    Pty::spawn_with_launcher_in(
                        &launcher,
                        &identity,
                        program,
                        args,
                        size,
                        &borrowed,
                        directory.as_deref(),
                    )
                };
                match spawn(&program, &args) {
                    Err(error)
                        if fallback && program != crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell() =>
                    {
                        let fallback = crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell();
                        crate::pty_output::say(&format!(
                            "'{program}' could not be launched ({error}); opening {fallback}"
                        ));
                        spawn(fallback, &[])
                    }
                    result => result,
                }
            });
        }
        let _ = (wake, reads);
        let spawn = |program, args: &[&str]| {
            Pty::spawn_with_env_in(program, args, request.size, request.env, request.directory)
        };
        let (pty, output) = match spawn(request.program, request.args) {
            Err(error)
                if request.fallback
                    && request.program != crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell() =>
            {
                let fallback = crate::shell_integration::fallback_shell();
                crate::pty_output::say(&format!(
                    "'{}' could not be launched ({error}); opening {fallback}",
                    request.program,
                ));
                spawn(fallback, &[])?
            }
            result => result?,
        };
        Ok((pty.into(), output))
    }

    pub(crate) fn live(&self) -> Option<&Pty> {
        self.live.as_ref()
    }
    pub(crate) fn pending(&self) -> bool {
        self.pending.is_some()
    }
    pub(crate) fn startup_pending(&self) -> bool {
        self.startup != 0
    }
    pub(crate) fn interpreted(&mut self, bytes: usize) {
        self.startup = self.startup.saturating_sub(bytes);
    }
    pub(crate) fn termination(&self) -> term_core::Termination {
        self.terminal
    }
    pub(crate) fn keeps_open(&self) -> bool {
        self.failure.is_some()
            || self.delivery_failed
            || self.pending()
            || self.termination() != term_core::Termination::Ordinary
    }
    pub(crate) fn notice(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        if self.dismissed {
            return None;
        }
        if self.failure.is_some() {
            return Some("This console's program could not be started.");
        }
        match self.termination() {
            term_core::Termination::Oom => {
                Some("This console was stopped because of memory pressure.")
            }
            term_core::Termination::Signal => Some("This console was terminated by a signal."),
            _ if self.delivery_failed => {
                Some("What you typed while this console was starting did not reach the program.")
            }
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn foreground_scope(&self) -> std::io::Result<ForegroundScope> {
        self.live
            .as_ref()
            .ok_or_else(|| std::io::Error::from(std::io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock))?
            .foreground_scope()
    }
    pub(crate) fn exit_success(&mut self) -> Option<bool> {
        self.live.as_mut()?.exit_success()
    }
    pub(crate) fn working_directory(&mut self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        self.live.as_mut()?.working_directory()
    }
    pub(crate) fn foreground_program(&mut self) -> Option<String> {
        self.live.as_mut()?.foreground_program()
    }
    pub(crate) fn ending(&self) -> Option<Ending> {
        self.live.as_ref()?.ending()
    }
    pub(crate) fn backlog(&self) -> Option<Backlog> {
        if let Some(live) = &self.live {
            return live.backlog();
        }
        self.input_since.map(|since| Backlog {
            bytes: self.input.len(),
            waiting: since.elapsed(),
            gave_up: self.failure.is_some(),
            refused: self.refused,
            refusals: self.refusals,
        })
    }
    pub(crate) fn resize(&mut self, size: Size) -> term_core::Result<()> {
        if !self.startup_pending()
            && let Some(live) = &mut self.live
        {
            live.resize(size)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub(crate) fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> term_core::Result<()> {
        if !self.startup_pending()
            && self.input.is_empty()
            && let Some(live) = &mut self.live
        {
            return live.write(bytes);
        }
        if self.pending.is_none() && self.live.is_none() {
            return Err("the console could not be started".into());
        }
        // Match D60: accept one paste whole, refuse subsequent input while the queue is full.
        if self.input.len() >= 256 * 1024 {
            self.refused = true;
            self.refusals += 1;
            return Err(
                "the pending console's input queue is full; input did not reach the program".into(),
            );
        }
        if !bytes.is_empty() {
            self.input_since.get_or_insert_with(std::time::Instant::now);
        }
        self.refused = false;
        self.input.extend_from_slice(bytes);
        Ok(())
    }

    fn poll(&mut self) -> Option<(Receiver<Vec<u8>>, Vec<u8>, crate::control::ReadActivity)> {
        let result = match self.pending.as_ref()?.result.try_recv() {
            Ok(result) => result,
            Err(TryRecvError::Empty) => return None,
            Err(TryRecvError::Disconnected) => Err("the console launch worker stopped".into()),
        };
        let pending = self.pending.take()?;
        match result {
            Ok(Ready {
                spawned: (pty, output),
                startup,
            }) => {
                self.live = Some(pty);
                self.startup = startup.len();
                Some((output, startup, pending.reads))
            }
            Err(error) => {
                self.failure = Some(error.to_string());
                self.input.clear();
                None
            }
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn complete(pane: &mut crate::Pane) {
    let Some((output, startup, reads)) = pane.pty.poll() else {
        if pane.pty.failure.is_some() && pane.restoration.resuming {
            pane.restoration.failed = true;
            pane.restoration.resuming = false;
            pane.restoration.fresh_shell_required = true;
        }
        return;
    };
    let wake = pane.restoration.wake.clone();
    pane.pty_output = crate::pty_output::PtyOutput::seeded(output, startup, move || {
        if let Some(wake) = &wake {
            wake();
        }
    });
    pane.restoration.scope_unavailable = false;
    if pane.control.is_some() {
        return;
    }
    let callback = pane.restoration.route.callback();
    let inspector = pane
        .pty
        .foreground_scope()
        .map(crate::panel::provenance_route::inspector)
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| crate::panel::provenance_route::unavailable());
    pane.control = pane.panel.as_ref().and_then(|dir| {
        let wake = pane.restoration.wake.clone();
        crate::control::open_with_authority(
            dir,
            &pane.panel_state,
            reads,
            Some(callback),
            pane.pty
                .foreground_scope()
                .ok()
                .map(crate::panel::overlay::Authority::new),
            inspector,
            move || {
                if let Some(wake) = &wake {
                    wake();
                }
            },
            pane.pty
                .context
                .as_ref()
                .and_then(crate::opening::control_hwnd),
        )
    });
    pane.restoration.scope_unavailable = false;
}

pub(crate) fn silences(process: &Process) -> crate::pty_output::Silences {
    process
        .live()
        .map(crate::pty_output::Silences::of)
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

impl crate::Pane {
    /// Applies the size the last UI pass measured.
    ///
    /// **The order between the two halves is not indifferent: the grid first and the program
    /// after.** As soon as the program knows the new size it starts sending bytes meant for it, and
    /// if the grid were still the old one they would be interpreted against the wrong dimensions.
    pub(crate) fn resize(&mut self) {
        crate::pty_output::resize(self);
    }

    /// Whether the program finished **well** and nothing of its own is left to show.
    ///
    /// **It waits for the queue to be empty**: dying does not erase what the program wrote before
    /// doing so, and giving the pane up for finished with a queue pending would eat precisely the
    /// end, which is the part that was being looked at.
    ///
    /// **What «finished» means is a decision and it lives outside this file**, in the module that
    /// owns the signal it rests on: see [`crate::pty_output::is_finished`]. **The ending is asked of the
    /// pseudoterminal** and not of anything the bridge keeps, so it does not depend on the pump
    /// having run this frame — which the queue, the other half of the question, does by definition.
    pub(crate) fn is_finished(&self, queue_left: bool) -> bool {
        crate::resume::finished(self, queue_left)
    }
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Cost {
    pub calls: usize,
    pub total: std::time::Duration,
}

pub(crate) fn refresh(pane: &mut crate::Pane) {
    let before = std::time::Instant::now();
    complete(pane);
    pane.pty.terminal = pane.pty.live.as_ref().map_or_else(
        || {
            if pane.pty.pending() {
                term_core::Termination::Pending
            } else {
                term_core::Termination::Ordinary
            }
        },
        Pty::termination,
    );
    if pane.pty.live.is_some()
        && !pane.pty_output.is_alive()
        && pane.pty.termination() == term_core::Termination::Pending
        && let Some(ctx) = &pane.pty.context
    {
        ctx.request_repaint_after(std::time::Duration::from_millis(20));
    }
    pane.pty.cost += before.elapsed();
}

pub(crate) fn take_cost(process: &mut Process) -> std::time::Duration {
    std::mem::take(&mut process.cost)
}

pub(crate) fn finish_input(pane: &mut crate::Pane) {
    if pane.pty.startup_pending() || pane.pty.live.is_none() || pane.pty.input.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let before = std::time::Instant::now();
    let input = std::mem::take(&mut pane.pty.input);
    if let Err(error) = pane
        .pty
        .live
        .as_mut()
        .expect("installed console")
        .write(&input)
    {
        pane.pty.delivery_failed = true;
        crate::pty_output::say(&format!(
            "pending console input could not be delivered: {error}"
        ));
    }
    pane.pty.input_since = None;
    pane.pty.cost += before.elapsed();
}

#[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", test))]
struct CompletionWake(std::sync::Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>);
#[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", test))]
impl Drop for CompletionWake {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        (self.0)();
    }
}

#[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", test))]
fn start_worker(
    wake: std::sync::Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>,
    reads: crate::control::ReadActivity,
    operation: impl FnOnce() -> term_core::Result<Spawned> + Send + 'static,
) -> term_core::Result<(Process, Receiver<Vec<u8>>)> {
    let (sender, result) = channel();
    let (output_open, output) = channel();
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("console-launch".into())
        .spawn(move || {
            let _completed = CompletionWake(wake);
            // On receiver cancellation the undelivered child is shut down on this worker.
            let deliver = sender;
            let _ = deliver.send(operation().map(prepare));
        })?;
    Ok((
        Process {
            live: None,
            terminal: term_core::Termination::Pending,
            cost: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
            context: None,
            pending: Some(Pending {
                result,
                _output_open: output_open,
                reads,
            }),
            input: Vec::new(),
            startup: 0,
            input_since: None,
            refused: false,
            refusals: 0,
            failure: None,
            delivery_failed: false,
            dismissed: false,
        },
        output,
    ))
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub(crate) mod tests;
```

### crates/app/src/opening.rs

```
//! How a window comes into being: with the tabs it is asked for, or with the ones it left behind.
//!
//! **Three methods and one subject.** Building a window is not what a window *does* — it is what
//! somebody does to one — and it reads as its own vocabulary: how many tabs, which consoles, what
//! the session file said and what happens when it says nothing understandable. Everything about
//! running is in [`crate`]; everything about starting is here.
//!
//! It came out of the window's core under **D28**, which gives an `impl` block a ceiling of 300
//! production lines and records what that file is at so the number can only go down. **It went out
//! ahead of the delivery that needed the room** — the milestone that gives the panel buttons — which
//! is the order that entry's own prose asks for.

use crate::painter::CellMetrics;
use crate::*;
use eframe::egui;

#[cfg(all(test, unix))]
mod codex_tests;

pub(crate) fn native_window(
    cc: &eframe::CreationContext<'_>,
    with_what: OpenWith,
    size: Option<egui::Vec2>,
    persistence: session::Persistence,
    measuring: bool,
) -> term_core::Result<Window> {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        use raw_window_handle::{HasWindowHandle, RawWindowHandle};
        let hwnd = cc
            .window_handle()
            .ok()
            .and_then(|handle| match handle.as_raw() {
                RawWindowHandle::Win32(handle) => Some(handle.hwnd.get()),
                _ => None,
            });
        if let Some(hwnd) = hwnd {
            cc.egui_ctx.data_mut(|data| {
                data.insert_temp(egui::Id::new("ivy-panel-control-hwnd"), hwnd);
            });
        }
    }
    chrome::prepare(&cc.egui_ctx);
    let mut window = Window::opening(
        &cc.egui_ctx,
        with_what,
        &the_users_shell(),
        size,
        persistence,
    )?;
    if !measuring {
        window.clipboard = clipboard::Service::new(cc.egui_ctx.clone());
    }
    Ok(window)
}

pub(crate) fn control_hwnd(ctx: &egui::Context) -> Option<isize> {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        ctx.data(|data| data.get_temp(egui::Id::new("ivy-panel-control-hwnd")))
    }
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    {
        let _ = ctx;
        None
    }
}

#[cfg(all(test, windows))]
mod windows_control_tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::ptr::{null, null_mut};

    #[test]
    fn windows_control_native_wake_posts_without_waiting_for_dispatch() {
        use windows_sys::Win32::{
            System::LibraryLoader::GetModuleHandleW,
            UI::WindowsAndMessaging::{
                CreateWindowExW, DefWindowProcW, DestroyWindow, MSG, PM_REMOVE, PeekMessageW,
                RegisterClassW, UnregisterClassW, WM_PAINT, WNDCLASSW,
            },
        };

        let class_name: Vec<u16> = format!("IvyControlWake{}", std::process::id())
            .encode_utf16()
            .chain(std::iter::once(0))
            .collect();
        let instance = unsafe { GetModuleHandleW(null()) };
        let class = WNDCLASSW {
            lpfnWndProc: Some(DefWindowProcW),
            hInstance: instance,
            lpszClassName: class_name.as_ptr(),
            ..Default::default()
        };
        assert_ne!(
            unsafe { RegisterClassW(&class) },
            0,
            "register native test window"
        );
        let hwnd = unsafe {
            CreateWindowExW(
                0,
                class_name.as_ptr(),
                class_name.as_ptr(),
                0,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                (-3isize) as _,
                null_mut(),
                instance,
                null(),
            )
        };
        assert!(!hwnd.is_null(), "create native message window");
        let wake = panel_control::WindowsWake::from_hwnd(hwnd as isize).unwrap();
        wake.post().expect("post native wake without dispatch");
        let mut message = MSG::default();
        assert_ne!(
            unsafe { PeekMessageW(&mut message, hwnd, WM_PAINT, WM_PAINT, PM_REMOVE) },
            0,
            "the posted WM_PAINT is queued while the window thread has not dispatched it"
        );
        wake.revoke();
        assert!(wake.post().is_err(), "revoked HWND rejects later posts");
        unsafe {
            DestroyWindow(hwnd);
            UnregisterClassW(class_name.as_ptr(), instance);
        }
    }
}

    pub(crate) fn new_counting_reads(
        ctx: &egui::Context,

    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn with_panel(

    ) -> term_core::Result<Self> {
```
